# Image prompts

697 of 697 cards still need a picture. This file is generated by `npm run sync`; edit a card's `visual` text in `content/` to change its prompt.

## How to use

1. Paste a prompt below into ChatGPT and generate the image.
2. Save it into the `image-inbox/` folder using the card id as the file name, for example `sci-03.png`.
3. Run `npm run images`. It resizes and compresses everything in the inbox into `src/assets/cards/`.
4. Run `npm run sync` to refresh this list.

Tip: to keep the series consistent, start a ChatGPT conversation with "I will send a series of illustration prompts. Keep the same art style across all of them." and then send prompts one at a time.

## Self-Development

### self-spaced-deadlines · Deadlines you set yourself help, but evenly spaced ones help more

Save as `self-spaced-deadlines.png`

```text
Illustration of a long road at dusk with three evenly spaced glowing milestones leading toward a distant finish gate. Colour palette: burnt orange, ember and deep brown, on a dark background. Style: rich editorial illustration, painterly digital art with bold simplified shapes, dramatic cinematic lighting and a subtle film grain. No text, letters, numbers, logos, borders or watermarks anywhere in the image. Format: vertical 2:3 portrait, 1024 x 1536 pixels. Centre the main subject and place it about one third of the way down. Leave a little plain background above it. Keep the bottom 40% of the image dark, simple and free of detail, because it fades into a text panel.
```
